ochal chal qochdy tshedy oshtar ochtor yshor chdyor dyom
oshor qoched kchdy chordar daiin ar ochal aiiror shog
daiiirom aiiiror chkaiin alkar ar aiiirom karar ychdy ed
shdolor qoshed shsam
pshsam dashpdy cheeor yshdy chedy aiin shar odal chpom
oshsam ochpom xor cheeor or chalkar aiiirol alair ochkog
shsam shsam kom
